For pacing multi-step work, use principle-take-small-verified-steps. ([Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills](https://github.com/Med-Karim-Ben-Boubaker/karim-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tdd) · ★1)
- **tdd**: Use when implementing any feature or bugfix, before writing implementation code - guides test-driven development across .NET (xUnit), Flutter (test), and Web (Vitest) ([meminozturkk/synapse-agents](https://github.com/meminozturkk/synapse-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/ecc-lite/skills/tdd) · ★1)
- **tdd**: Use when implementing any feature or bug fix — before writing any production code ([mezivillager/hacer](https://github.com/mezivillager/hacer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/tdd) · ★1)
